[#udb:doc:inst:vabd_vv]
= vabd.vv

Synopsis::
Vector Single-Width Signed Integer Absolute Difference

Mnemonic::
----
vabd.vv vd, vs2, vs1, vm
----

Encoding::
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x57,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "vd","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": 0x2,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "vs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "vs2","type":4},{"bits":1,"name": "vm","type":4},{"bits":6,"name": 0x11,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the element-wise absolute difference of two signed integer SEW-bit
source operands vs1 and vs2 and writes the result to vd according to mask vm.

Reserved when SEW is neither 8 nor 16.



Decode Variables::

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<1> vm = $encoding[25];
Bits<5> vs2 = $encoding[24:20];
Bits<5> vs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> vd = $encoding[11:7];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
VectorState state = xref:#udb-function-vector_state[vector_state]pass:[(]);
if pass:[(]state.sew != 8 && state.sew != 16) {
  xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
VectorLmulType lmul_type = state.lmul_type;
XReg vlen = VLEN;
XReg vlmax;
if pass:[(]lmul_type == VectorLmulType::Multiply) {
  vlmax = (vlen << state.log2_lmul) >> state.log2_sew;
} else {
  vlmax = (vlen >> state.log2_lmul) >> state.log2_sew;
}
for pass:[(]U32 i = CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vstart[vstart]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vstart-VALUE[VALUE]; i < CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vl[vl]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vl-VALUE[VALUE]; i++) {
  U32 start_bit_pos = i * state.sew;
  U32 end_bit_pos = start_bit_pos pass:[+] state.sew - 1;
  if pass:[(]vm == 1'b1 || V[0][i:i] == 1'b1) {
    if pass:[(]$signed+++(+++V[vs2][end_bit_pos:start_bit_pos]) >= $signed+++(+++V[vs1][end_bit_pos:start_bit_pos])) {
      V[vd][end_bit_pos:start_bit_pos] = V[vs2][end_bit_pos:start_bit_pos] - V[vs1][end_bit_pos:start_bit_pos];
    } else {
      V[vd][end_bit_pos:start_bit_pos] = V[vs1][end_bit_pos:start_bit_pos] - V[vs2][end_bit_pos:start_bit_pos];
    }
  }
}
CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vstart[vstart]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vstart-VALUE[VALUE] = 0;
----

[#udb:doc:inst:vabs_v]
= vabs.v

Synopsis::
Vector Single-Width Signed Integer Absolute

Mnemonic::
----
vabs.v vd, vs2, vm
----

[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....

result in vector register vd according to mask vm.

vabs.v supports SEW of 8, 16, and 32 when Zve32x is implemented.
When Zve64x is implemented, vabs.v also supports SEW of 64.

[#udb:doc:inst:vfext_vf2]
= vfext.vf2

Synopsis::
Vector OFP4 to OFP8 widening conversion

Mnemonic::
----
vfext.vf2 vd, vs2, vm
----

Encoding::
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x57,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "vd","type":4},{"bits":8,"name": 0xb2,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "vs2","type":4},{"bits":1,"name": "vm","type":4},{"bits":6,"name": 0x12,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Converts a vector of OFP4 E2M1 values in vector register vs2 (EEW=4,
EMUL=LMUL/2) to a vector of OFP8 E4M3 values in vector register vd
(EEW=8, EMUL=LMUL), according to mask vm. Defined only for SEW=8,
altfmt=0, and LMUL >= 1/4. No rounding occurs, and no floating-point
exception flags are set. This instruction does not access the fcsr
and is not considered a floating-point instruction for the purposes
of the floating-point context status (FS) setting.



Decode Variables::

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<1> vm = $encoding[25];
Bits<5> vs2 = $encoding[24:20];
Bits<5> vd = $encoding[11:7];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

----

[#udb:doc:inst:vwabda_vv]
= vwabda.vv

Synopsis::
Vector Widening Signed Integer Absolute Difference and Accumulate, Overwrite Addend

Mnemonic::
----
vwabda.vv vd, vs2, vs1, vm
----

Encoding::
[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x57,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "vd","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": 0x2,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "vs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "vs2","type":4},{"bits":1,"name": "vm","type":4},{"bits":6,"name": 0x15,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the element-wise absolute difference of two signed integer SEW-bit
source operands vs1 and vs2, and accumulates the results into the elements of
a 2*SEW-bit operand vd.

Reserved when SEW is neither 8 nor 16.



Decode Variables::

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<1> vm = $encoding[25];
Bits<5> vs2 = $encoding[24:20];
Bits<5> vs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> vd = $encoding[11:7];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
VectorState state = xref:#udb-function-vector_state[vector_state]pass:[(]);
if pass:[(]state.sew != 8 && state.sew != 16) {
  xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
VectorLmulType lmul_type = state.lmul_type;
XReg vlen = VLEN;
XReg vlmax;
if pass:[(]lmul_type == VectorLmulType::Multiply) {
  vlmax = (vlen << state.log2_lmul) >> state.log2_sew;
} else {
  vlmax = (vlen >> state.log2_lmul) >> state.log2_sew;
}
U32 sew2 = state.sew pass:[+] state.sew;
for pass:[(]U32 i = CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vstart[vstart]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vstart-VALUE[VALUE]; i < CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vl[vl]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vl-VALUE[VALUE]; i++) {
  U32 src_start = i * state.sew;
  U32 src_end = src_start pass:[+] state.sew - 1;
  U32 dst_start = i * sew2;
  U32 dst_end = dst_start pass:[+] sew2 - 1;
  if pass:[(]vm == 1'b1 || V[0][i:i] == 1'b1) {
    if pass:[(]$signed+++(+++V[vs2][src_end:src_start]) >= $signed+++(+++V[vs1][src_end:src_start])) {
      V[vd][dst_end:dst_start] = V[vd][dst_end:dst_start] pass:[+] (V[vs2][src_end:src_start] - V[vs1][src_end:src_start]);
    } else {
      V[vd][dst_end:dst_start] = V[vd][dst_end:dst_start] pass:[+] (V[vs1][src_end:src_start] - V[vs2][src_end:src_start]);
    }
  }
}
CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vstart[vstart]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vstart-VALUE[VALUE] = 0;
----


Included in::
--

| Extension | Version


|===

[wavedrom, ,svg,subs='attributes',width="100%"]
....
{"reg":[{"bits":7,"name": 0x57,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "vd","type":4},{"bits":3,"name": 0x2,"type":2},{"bits":5,"name": "vs1","type":4},{"bits":5,"name": "vs2","type":4},{"bits":1,"name": "vm","type":4},{"bits":6,"name": 0x16,"type":2}]}
....

Description::
Computes the element-wise absolute difference of two unsigned integer SEW-bit
source operands vs1 and vs2, and accumulates the results into the elements of
a 2*SEW-bit operand vd.

Reserved when SEW is neither 8 nor 16.



Decode Variables::

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<1> vm = $encoding[25];
Bits<5> vs2 = $encoding[24:20];
Bits<5> vs1 = $encoding[19:15];
Bits<5> vd = $encoding[11:7];
----


Operation::
[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
VectorState state = xref:#udb-function-vector_state[vector_state]pass:[(]);
if pass:[(]state.sew != 8 && state.sew != 16) {
  xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), $encoding);
}
VectorLmulType lmul_type = state.lmul_type;
XReg vlen = VLEN;
XReg vlmax;
if pass:[(]lmul_type == VectorLmulType::Multiply) {
  vlmax = (vlen << state.log2_lmul) >> state.log2_sew;
} else {
  vlmax = (vlen >> state.log2_lmul) >> state.log2_sew;
}
U32 sew2 = state.sew pass:[+] state.sew;
for pass:[(]U32 i = CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vstart[vstart]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vstart-VALUE[VALUE]; i < CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vl[vl]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vl-VALUE[VALUE]; i++) {
  U32 src_start = i * state.sew;
  U32 src_end = src_start pass:[+] state.sew - 1;
  U32 dst_start = i * sew2;
  U32 dst_end = dst_start pass:[+] sew2 - 1;
  if pass:[(]vm == 1'b1 || V[0][i:i] == 1'b1) {
    if pass:[(]V[vs2][src_end:src_start] >= V[vs1][src_end:src_start]) {
      V[vd][dst_end:dst_start] = V[vd][dst_end:dst_start] pass:[+] (V[vs2][src_end:src_start] - V[vs1][src_end:src_start]);
    } else {
      V[vd][dst_end:dst_start] = V[vd][dst_end:dst_start] pass:[+] (V[vs1][src_end:src_start] - V[vs2][src_end:src_start]);
    }
  }
}
CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vstart[vstart]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vstart-VALUE[VALUE] = 0;
----

[#udb-function-access_check]
=== access_check
Checks if the physical address paddr is able to access memory, and raises
the appropriate exception if not.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `void`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<PHYS_ADDR_WIDTH>`
! paddr
! 1
h! `U32`
! access_size
! 2
h! `XReg`
! vaddr
! 3
h! `MemoryOperation`
! type
! 4
h! `ExceptionCode`
! fault_type
! 5
h! `PrivilegeMode`
! from_mode
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]paddr > ((1 pass:[`]<< PHYS_ADDR_WIDTH) - access_size)) {
  xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]fault_type, from_mode, vaddr);
}
if pass:[(]NUM_PMP_ENTRIES > 0) {
  if pass:[(]!xref:#udb-function-pmp_check[pmp_check]pass:[(]paddr[PHYS_ADDR_WIDTH - 1:0], access_size, type)) {
    xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]fault_type, from_mode, vaddr);
  }
}
----

[#udb-function-check_zcmt_enabled]
=== check_zcmt_enabled
If the Smstateen extension is implemented, then bit 2 in `mstateen0`, `sstateen0`, and `hstateen0` is
implemented. If bit 2 of a controlling `stateen0` CSR is zero, then access to the `jvt` CSR and execution
of a xref:#udb-insn-cm_jalt[`cm.jalt`] or xref:#udb-insn-cm_jt[`cm.jt`] instruction by a lower privilege level results in an illegal-instruction trap (or, if
appropriate, a virtual-instruction trap).
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `void`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<INSTR_ENC_SIZE>`
! encoding
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(](xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) != PrivilegeMode::M && xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::Smstateen) && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstateen0[mstateen0]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstateen0-JVT[JVT] == 1'b0) || (xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::U && xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::Ssstateen) && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-sstateen0[sstateen0]].xref:#udb-csrfield-sstateen0-JVT[JVT] == 1'b0)) {
  xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), encoding);
} else if pass:[(](xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::VS && xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::Ssstateen) && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstateen0[hstateen0]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstateen0-JVT[JVT] == 1'b0) || (xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::VU && xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::Ssstateen) && (CSR[xref:#udb-csr-sstateen0[sstateen0]].xref:#udb-csrfield-sstateen0-JVT[JVT] == 1'b0 || CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstateen0[hstateen0]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstateen0-JVT[JVT] == 1'b0))) {
  xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::VirtualInstruction, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), encoding);
}
----

[#udb-function-compatible_mode?]
=== compatible_mode?
Returns true if +target_mode+ is more privileged than +actual_mode+.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `Boolean`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `PrivilegeMode`
! target_mode
! 1
h! `PrivilegeMode`
! actual_mode
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]target_mode == PrivilegeMode::M) {
  return actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::M;
} else if pass:[(]target_mode == PrivilegeMode::S) {
  return (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::M) || (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::S);
} else if pass:[(]target_mode == PrivilegeMode::U) {
  return (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::M) || (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::S) || (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::U);
} else if pass:[(]target_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS) {
  return (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::M) || (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::S) || (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS);
} else if pass:[(]target_mode == PrivilegeMode::VU) {
  return (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::M) || (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::S) || (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS) || (actual_mode == PrivilegeMode::VU);
}
return false;
----

[#udb-function-count_leading_zeros]
=== count_leading_zeros
Returns the number of leading 0 bits before the most-significant 1 bit of +value+,
or N if value is zero.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `XReg`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `U32`
! N
! 1
h! `XReg`
! value
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
for pass:[(]U32 i = 0; i < N; i++) {
  if pass:[(]value[N - 1 - i] == 1) {
    return i;
  }
}
return N;
----

[#udb-function-creg2reg]
=== creg2reg
Maps a C register index (_e.g._, `rs1'` in the specification) to an X register index. From the
specification:

.Registers specified by the three-bit _rs1_&#x2032;, _rs2_&#x2032;, and _rd_&#x2032; fields of the CIW, CL, CS, CA, and CB formats.

!`s0` !`s1` !`a0` !`a1` !`a2` !`a3` !`a4`!`a5`

[#udb-function-current_translation_mode]
=== current_translation_mode
Returns the current first-stage translation mode for an explicit load or store
from +mode+ given the machine state (e.g., value of `satp` or `vsatp` csr).

Returns SatpMode::Reserved if the setting found in `satp` or `vsatp` is invalid.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `SatpMode`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `PrivilegeMode`
! mode
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
PrivilegeMode effective_mode = xref:#udb-function-effective_ldst_mode[effective_ldst_mode]pass:[(]);
if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::M) {
  return SatpMode::Bare;
}
if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-H[H] == 1'b1) {
  if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS || effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VU) {
    Bits<4> mode_val = CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vsatp[vsatp]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vsatp-MODE[MODE];
    if pass:[(]mode_val == $bits(SatpMode::Bare)) {
      return SatpMode::Bare;
    } else if pass:[(]mode_val == $bits(SatpMode::Sv32)) {
      if pass:[(]MXLEN == 64) {
        if pass:[(](effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS) && (CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-VSXL[VSXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN32))) {
          return SatpMode::Reserved;
        }
        if pass:[(](effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VU) && (CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vsstatus[vsstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vsstatus-UXL[UXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN32))) {
          return SatpMode::Reserved;
        }
      }
      if pass:[(]!SV32_VSMODE_TRANSLATION) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      return SatpMode::Sv32;
    } else if pass:[(](MXLEN == 64) && (mode_val == $bits(SatpMode::Sv39))) {
      if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-VSXL[VSXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VU && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vsstatus[vsstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vsstatus-UXL[UXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      if pass:[(]!SV39_VSMODE_TRANSLATION) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      return SatpMode::Sv39;
    } else if pass:[(](MXLEN == 64) && (mode_val == $bits(SatpMode::Sv48))) {
      if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-VSXL[VSXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VU && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vsstatus[vsstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vsstatus-UXL[UXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      if pass:[(]!SV48_VSMODE_TRANSLATION) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      return SatpMode::Sv48;
    } else if pass:[(](MXLEN == 64) && (mode_val == $bits(SatpMode::Sv57))) {
      if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-VSXL[VSXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::VU && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vsstatus[vsstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vsstatus-UXL[UXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      if pass:[(]!SV57_VSMODE_TRANSLATION) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      return SatpMode::Sv57;
    } else {
      return SatpMode::Reserved;
    }
  } else {
    return SatpMode::Reserved;
  }
} else if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-S[S] == 1'b1) {
  xref:#udb-function-assert[assert]pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::S || effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::U, "unexpected priv mode");
  Bits<4> mode_val = CSR[xref:#udb-csr-satp[satp]].xref:#udb-csrfield-satp-MODE[MODE];
  if pass:[(]mode_val == $bits(SatpMode::Bare)) {
    return SatpMode::Bare;
  } else if pass:[(]mode_val == $bits(SatpMode::Sv32)) {
    if pass:[(]MXLEN == 64) {
      if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::S && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-SXL[SXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN32)) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
      if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::U && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-sstatus[sstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-sstatus-UXL[UXL] != $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN32)) {
        return SatpMode::Reserved;
      }
    }

      if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::H) && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-H[H] == 1 && xref:#udb-function-mpv[mpv]pass:[(]) == 0b1) {
        return PrivilegeMode::VU;

    } else if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-S[S] == 1 && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-MPP[MPP] == 0b01) {
      if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::H) && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-H[H] == 1 && xref:#udb-function-mpv[mpv]pass:[(]) == 0b1) {

[#udb-function-exception_handling_mode]
=== exception_handling_mode
Returns the target privilege mode that will handle synchronous exception `exception_code`
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `PrivilegeMode`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `ExceptionCode`
! exception_code
!===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::M) {
  return PrivilegeMode::M;
} else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::S) && ((xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::HS) || (xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::U))) {
  if pass:[(]($bits(CSR[xref:#udb-csr-medeleg[CSR[medeleg]]]) & (MXLEN'1 << $bits(exception_code))) != 0) {
    return PrivilegeMode::HS;
  } else {
    return PrivilegeMode::M;
  }
} else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::H) && ((xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::VS) || (xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::VU))) {
  if pass:[(]($bits(CSR[xref:#udb-csr-medeleg[CSR[medeleg]]]) & (MXLEN'1 << $bits(exception_code))) != 0) {
    if pass:[(]($bits(CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hedeleg[CSR[hedeleg]]]) & (MXLEN'1 << $bits(exception_code))) != 0) {
      return PrivilegeMode::VS;
    } else {
      return PrivilegeMode::HS;
    }
  } else {
    return PrivilegeMode::M;
  }
} else {
  xref:#udb-function-unreachable[unreachable]pass:[(]);
}

  Softfloat_mulAdd_subC:    (a * b) - c  (negate c)
  Softfloat_mulAdd_subProd: -(a * b) + c (negate product)

Bits<16> expA = xref:#udb-function-expF32UI[expF32UI]pass:[(]a);

if pass:[(]expA == 8'hFF) {
  if pass:[(]fracA != 0) {
    return SP_CANONICAL_NAN;
  }
  if pass:[(]signA == 0) {
    return a;
  }
  xref:#udb-function-set_fp_flag[set_fp_flag]pass:[(]FpFlag::NV);
  return SP_CANONICAL_NAN;
}
if pass:[(]signA == 1) {
  if pass:[(](expA == 0) && (fracA == 0)) {
    return a;
  }
  xref:#udb-function-set_fp_flag[set_fp_flag]pass:[(]FpFlag::NV);
  return SP_CANONICAL_NAN;
}
if pass:[(]expA == 0) {
  if pass:[(]fracA == 0) {
    return a;
  }
  ExpAndSig norm = xref:#udb-function-softfloat_normSubnormalF32Sig[softfloat_normSubnormalF32Sig]pass:[(]fracA);
  expA = norm.exp;
  fracA = norm.sig;
}
Bits<16> adjExp = expA - 16'h007F;
Bits<16> expZ = ((adjExp >> 1) ^ (adjExp & 16'h8000)) pass:[+] 16'h007E;

    }

return xref:#udb-function-softfloat_roundPackToF32[softfloat_roundPackToF32]pass:[(]0, expZ, sigZ, mode);

[#udb-function-misaligned_is_atomic?]
=== misaligned_is_atomic?
Returns true if an access starting at +physical_address+ that is +N+ bits long is atomic.

This function takes into account any Atomicity Granule PMAs, so *it should not be used
for load-reserved/store-conditional*, since those PMAs do not apply to those accesses.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `Boolean`
h| Arguments
a|

return false if (MISALIGNED_MAX_ATOMICITY_GRANULE_SIZE == 0);
if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-pma_applies?[pma_applies?]pass:[(]PmaAttribute::MAG16, physical_address, N) && xref:#udb-function-in_naturally_aligned_region?[in_naturally_aligned_region?]pass:[(]128, physical_address, N)) {
  return true;
} else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-pma_applies?[pma_applies?]pass:[(]PmaAttribute::MAG8, physical_address, N) && xref:#udb-function-in_naturally_aligned_region?[in_naturally_aligned_region?]pass:[(]64, physical_address, N)) {
  return true;
} else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-pma_applies?[pma_applies?]pass:[(]PmaAttribute::MAG4, physical_address, N) && xref:#udb-function-in_naturally_aligned_region?[in_naturally_aligned_region?]pass:[(]32, physical_address, N)) {
  return true;
} else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-pma_applies?[pma_applies?]pass:[(]PmaAttribute::MAG2, physical_address, N) && xref:#udb-function-in_naturally_aligned_region?[in_naturally_aligned_region?]pass:[(]16, physical_address, N)) {
  return true;
} else {
  return false;
}

[#udb-function-mtval_for]
=== mtval_for
Given an exception code and a *legal* non-zero value for mtval,
returns the value to be written in mtval considering implementation options
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `XReg`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `ExceptionCode`
! exception_code
! 1
h! `XReg`
! tval
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::Breakpoint) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_BREAKPOINT ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::LoadAddressMisaligned) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_LOAD_MISALIGNED ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAddressMisaligned) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_STORE_AMO_MISALIGNED ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::InstructionAddressMisaligned) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_INSTRUCTION_MISALIGNED ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::LoadAccessFault) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_LOAD_ACCESS_FAULT ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAccessFault) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_STORE_AMO_ACCESS_FAULT ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::InstructionAccessFault) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_INSTRUCTION_ACCESS_FAULT ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::LoadPageFault) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_LOAD_PAGE_FAULT ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::StoreAmoPageFault) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_STORE_AMO_PAGE_FAULT ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::InstructionPageFault) {
  return REPORT_VA_IN_MTVAL_ON_INSTRUCTION_PAGE_FAULT ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction) {
  return REPORT_ENCODING_IN_MTVAL_ON_ILLEGAL_INSTRUCTION ? tval : 0;
} else if pass:[(]exception_code == ExceptionCode::SoftwareCheck) {
  return tval;
} else {
  return 0;
}
----

[#udb-function-raise]
=== raise
Raise synchronous exception number `exception_code`.

The exception may be imprecise, and will cause execution to enter an
unpredictable state, if PRECISE_SYNCHRONOUS_EXCEPTIONS is false.

Otherwise, the exception will be precise.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `void`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `ExceptionCode`
! exception_code
! 1
h! `PrivilegeMode`
! from_mode
! 2
h! `XReg`
! tval
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]!PRECISE_SYNCHRONOUS_EXCEPTIONS) {
  xref:#udb-function-unpredictable[unpredictable]pass:[(]"Imprecise synchronous exception");
} else {
  xref:#udb-function-raise_precise[raise_precise]pass:[(]exception_code, from_mode, tval);
}
----

[#udb-function-raise_precise]
=== raise_precise
Raise synchronous exception number `exception_code`.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `void`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `ExceptionCode`
! exception_code
! 1
h! `PrivilegeMode`
! from_mode
! 2
h! `XReg`
! tval
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
PrivilegeMode handling_mode = xref:#udb-function-exception_handling_mode[exception_handling_mode]pass:[(]exception_code);
if pass:[(]handling_mode == PrivilegeMode::M) {
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mepc[mepc]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mepc-PC[PC] = $pc;
  if pass:[(]!xref:#udb-function-mtval_readonly?[mtval_readonly?]pass:[(])) {
    CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mtval[mtval]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mtval-VALUE[VALUE] = xref:#udb-function-mtval_for[mtval_for]pass:[(]exception_code, tval);
  }
  $pc = {CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mtvec[mtvec]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mtvec-BASE[BASE], 2'b00};
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mcause[mcause]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mcause-INT[INT] = 1'b0;
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mcause[mcause]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mcause-CODE[CODE] = $bits(exception_code);
  if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::H) && CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-H[H] == 1) {
    CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mtval2[mtval2]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mtval2-VALUE[VALUE] = 0;
    CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mtinst[mtinst]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mtinst-VALUE[VALUE] = 0;
    if pass:[(]from_mode == PrivilegeMode::VU || from_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS) {
      if pass:[(]MXLEN == 32) {
        CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatush[mstatush]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatush-MPV[MPV] = 1;
      } else {
        CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-MPV[MPV] = 1;
      }
    } else {
      if pass:[(]MXLEN == 32) {
        CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatush[mstatush]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatush-MPV[MPV] = 0;
      } else {
        CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-MPV[MPV] = 0;
      }
    }
  }
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-MPP[MPP] = $bits(from_mode);
} else if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-S[S] == 1 && (handling_mode == PrivilegeMode::S)) {
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-sepc[sepc]].xref:#udb-csrfield-sepc-PC[PC] = $pc;
  if pass:[(]!xref:#udb-function-stval_readonly?[stval_readonly?]pass:[(])) {
    CSR[xref:#udb-csr-stval[stval]].xref:#udb-csrfield-stval-VALUE[VALUE] = xref:#udb-function-stval_for[stval_for]pass:[(]exception_code, tval);
  }
  $pc = {CSR[xref:#udb-csr-stvec[stvec]].xref:#udb-csrfield-stvec-BASE[BASE], 2'b00};
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-scause[scause]].xref:#udb-csrfield-scause-INT[INT] = 1'b0;
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-scause[scause]].xref:#udb-csrfield-scause-CODE[CODE] = $bits(exception_code);
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-SPP[SPP] = $bits(from_mode)[0];
  if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-H[H] == 1) {
    CSR[xref:#udb-csr-htval[htval]].xref:#udb-csrfield-htval-VALUE[VALUE] = 0;
    CSR[xref:#udb-csr-htinst[htinst]].xref:#udb-csrfield-htinst-VALUE[VALUE] = 0;
    CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-SPV[SPV] = $bits(from_mode)[2];
    if pass:[(]from_mode == PrivilegeMode::VU || from_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS) {
      CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-SPV[SPV] = 1;
      if pass:[(]((exception_code == ExceptionCode::Breakpoint) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_BREAKPOINT)) || ((exception_code == ExceptionCode::LoadAddressMisaligned) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_LOAD_MISALIGNED)) || ((exception_code == ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAddressMisaligned) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_STORE_AMO_MISALIGNED)) || ((exception_code == ExceptionCode::InstructionAddressMisaligned) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_INSTRUCTION_MISALIGNED)) || ((exception_code == ExceptionCode::LoadAccessFault) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_LOAD_ACCESS_FAULT)) || ((exception_code == ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAccessFault) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_STORE_AMO_ACCESS_FAULT)) || ((exception_code == ExceptionCode::InstructionAccessFault) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_INSTRUCTION_ACCESS_FAULT)) || ((exception_code == ExceptionCode::LoadPageFault) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_LOAD_PAGE_FAULT)) || ((exception_code == ExceptionCode::StoreAmoPageFault) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_STORE_AMO_PAGE_FAULT)) || ((exception_code == ExceptionCode::InstructionPageFault) && (REPORT_VA_IN_STVAL_ON_INSTRUCTION_PAGE_FAULT))) {
        CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-GVA[GVA] = 1;
      } else {
        CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-GVA[GVA] = 0;
      }
      CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-SPVP[SPVP] = $bits(from_mode)[0];
    } else {
      CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-SPV[SPV] = 0;
      CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-GVA[GVA] = 0;
    }
  }
} else if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-H[H] == 1 && (handling_mode == PrivilegeMode::VS)) {
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vsepc[vsepc]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vsepc-PC[PC] = $pc;
  if pass:[(]!xref:#udb-function-vstval_readonly?[vstval_readonly?]pass:[(])) {
    CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vstval[vstval]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vstval-VALUE[VALUE] = xref:#udb-function-vstval_for[vstval_for]pass:[(]exception_code, tval);
  }
  $pc = {CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vstvec[vstvec]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vstvec-BASE[BASE], 2'b00};
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vscause[vscause]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vscause-INT[INT] = 1'b0;
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vscause[vscause]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vscause-CODE[CODE] = $bits(exception_code);
  CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vsstatus[vsstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vsstatus-SPP[SPP] = $bits(from_mode)[0];
}
xref:#udb-function-set_mode[set_mode]pass:[(]handling_mode);
xref:#udb-function-abort_current_instruction[abort_current_instruction]pass:[(]);

[#udb-function-read_memory]
=== read_memory
Read from virtual memory.

  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `XReg`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `U32`
! LEN
! 1
h! `XReg`
! virtual_address
! 2
h! `Bits<INSTR_ENC_SIZE>`
! encoding
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Boolean aligned = xref:#udb-function-is_naturally_aligned[is_naturally_aligned]pass:[(]LEN, virtual_address);
XReg physical_address;
if pass:[(]aligned) {
  return xref:#udb-function-read_memory_aligned[read_memory_aligned]pass:[(]LEN, virtual_address, encoding, 1'b0, 1'b0);
}
if pass:[(]MISALIGNED_MAX_ATOMICITY_GRANULE_SIZE > 0) {
  xref:#udb-function-assert[assert]pass:[(]MISALIGNED_LDST_EXCEPTION_PRIORITY == "low", "Invalid config: can't mix low-priority misaligned exceptions with large atomicity granule");
  physical_address = (CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-S[S] == 1) ? xref:#udb-function-translate[translate]pass:[(]virtual_address, MemoryOperation::Read, xref:#udb-function-effective_ldst_mode[effective_ldst_mode]pass:[(]), encoding).paddr : virtual_address;
  if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-misaligned_is_atomic?[misaligned_is_atomic?]pass:[(]LEN, physical_address)) {
    xref:#udb-function-access_check[access_check]pass:[(]physical_address, LEN, virtual_address, MemoryOperation::Read, ExceptionCode::LoadAccessFault, xref:#udb-function-effective_ldst_mode[effective_ldst_mode]pass:[(]));
    return xref:#udb-function-read_physical_memory[read_physical_memory]pass:[(]LEN, physical_address);
  }
}
if pass:[(]!MISALIGNED_LDST) {
  if pass:[(]MISALIGNED_LDST_EXCEPTION_PRIORITY == "low") {
    physical_address = (CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-S[S] == 1) ? xref:#udb-function-translate[translate]pass:[(]virtual_address, MemoryOperation::Read, xref:#udb-function-effective_ldst_mode[effective_ldst_mode]pass:[(]), encoding).paddr : virtual_address;
    xref:#udb-function-access_check[access_check]pass:[(]physical_address, LEN, virtual_address, MemoryOperation::Read, ExceptionCode::LoadAccessFault, xref:#udb-function-effective_ldst_mode[effective_ldst_mode]pass:[(]));
  }
  xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::LoadAddressMisaligned, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), virtual_address);
} else {
  if pass:[(]MISALIGNED_SPLIT_STRATEGY == "sequential_bytes") {
    XReg result = 0;
    for pass:[(]U32 I = 0; I < (LEN / 8); I++) {
      result = result | (xref:#udb-function-read_memory_aligned[read_memory_aligned]pass:[(]8, virtual_address pass:[+] I, encoding, 1'b0, 1'b0) pass:[`]<< (8 * I));
    }
    return result;
  } else if pass:[(]MISALIGNED_SPLIT_STRATEGY == "custom") {
    xref:#udb-function-unpredictable[unpredictable]pass:[(]"An implementation is free to break a misaligned access any way, leading to unpredictable behavior when any part of the misaligned access causes an exception");
    return 0;
  }
}
return 0;
----

[#udb-function-read_memory_aligned]
=== read_memory_aligned
Read from virtual memory using a known aligned address.

If +rl+ is 1, then the load also acts as a memory model release (applied after all
checks pass but before the physical read).
If +aq+ is 1, then the load also acts as a memory model acquire (applied after the read).
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `XReg`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `U32`
! LEN
! 1
h! `XReg`
! virtual_address
! 2
h! `Bits<INSTR_ENC_SIZE>`
! encoding
! 3
h! `Bits<1>`
! aq
! 4
h! `Bits<1>`
! rl
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
TranslationResult result;
if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-S[S] == 1) {
  result = xref:#udb-function-translate[translate]pass:[(]virtual_address, MemoryOperation::Read, xref:#udb-function-effective_ldst_mode[effective_ldst_mode]pass:[(]), encoding);
} else {
  result.paddr = virtual_address;
}
xref:#udb-function-access_check[access_check]pass:[(]result.paddr, LEN, virtual_address, MemoryOperation::Read, ExceptionCode::LoadAccessFault, xref:#udb-function-effective_ldst_mode[effective_ldst_mode]pass:[(]));
if pass:[(]rl == 1'b1) {
  xref:#udb-function-memory_model_release[memory_model_release]pass:[(]);
}
XReg value = xref:#udb-function-read_physical_memory[read_physical_memory]pass:[(]LEN, result.paddr);
if pass:[(]aq == 1'b1) {
  xref:#udb-function-memory_model_acquire[memory_model_acquire]pass:[(]);
}
return value;
----

[#udb-function-refresh_pending_interrupts]
=== refresh_pending_interrupts
refreshes the calculation of a pending interrupt

needs to be called after any state update that could change a pending interrupt. This includes:
   - CSR[mip]
   - CSR[mie]
   - CSR[mstatus].MIE
   - CSR[mstatus].SIE
   - CSR[vsstatus].SIE
   - CSR[mideleg]
   - CSR[sideleg]
   - CSR[hideleg]
   - CSR[hvip]
   - CSR[hgeip]
   - CSR[hgeie]
   - mode changes
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `void`
h| Arguments
a|

None

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<MXLEN> pending_ints = CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mip[CSR[mip]]].sw_read() & $bits(CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mie[CSR[mie]]]);
if pass:[(]pending_ints == 0) {
  pending_and_enabled_interrupts = 0;
  return ;
}
Boolean HAS_MIDELEG = xref:#udb-function-implemented_version?[implemented_version?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::S, "<= 1.9.1") || (xref:#udb-function-implemented_version?[implemented_version?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::S, "> 1.9.1") && xref:#udb-function-implemented_version?[implemented_version?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::Sm, "> 1.9.1"));
Bits<MXLEN> mmode_enabled_ints = ((xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::M) && (CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-MIE[MIE] == 1'b0)) ? 0 : ($bits(CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mie[CSR[mie]]]) & (HAS_MIDELEG ? ~$bits(CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mideleg[CSR[mideleg]]]) : ~MXLEN'0));
Bits<MXLEN> mmode_pending_and_enabled = pending_ints & mmode_enabled_ints;
if pass:[(]mmode_pending_and_enabled != 0) {
  pending_and_enabled_interrupts = mmode_pending_and_enabled;
  return ;
}
if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-S[S] == 1'b1) {
  Bits<MXLEN> smode_enabled_ints = ((xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::M) || (CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-SIE[SIE] == 1'b0)) ? 0 : $bits(CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mie[CSR[mie]]]) & ($bits(CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mideleg[CSR[mideleg]]]));
  Bits<MXLEN> smode_pending_and_enabled = pending_ints & smode_enabled_ints;
  if pass:[(]smode_pending_and_enabled != 0) {
    pending_and_enabled_interrupts = smode_pending_and_enabled;
    return ;
  }
}
pending_and_enabled_interrupts = 0;
----

[#udb-function-rm_to_mode]
=== rm_to_mode
Convert +rm+ to a RoundingMode.

+encoding+ is the full encoding of the instruction +rm+ comes from.

Will raise an IllegalInstruction exception if rm is a
reserved encoding.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `RoundingMode`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]

! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<3>`
! rm
! 1
h! `Bits<32>`
! encoding
!===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]rm == $bits(RoundingMode::RNE)) {
  return RoundingMode::RNE;
} else if pass:[(]rm == $bits(RoundingMode::RTZ)) {
  return RoundingMode::RTZ;
} else if pass:[(]rm == $bits(RoundingMode::RDN)) {
  return RoundingMode::RDN;
} else if pass:[(]rm == $bits(RoundingMode::RUP)) {
  return RoundingMode::RUP;
} else if pass:[(]rm == $bits(RoundingMode::RMM)) {
  return RoundingMode::RMM;
} else if pass:[(]rm == $bits(RoundingMode::DYN)) {
  return $enum(RoundingMode, CSR[xref:#udb-csr-fcsr[fcsr]].xref:#udb-csrfield-fcsr-FRM[FRM]);
} else {
  xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::IllegalInstruction, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), encoding);
}
----

[#udb-function-sext]
=== sext
Sign extend `value` starting at `first_extended_bit`.

Bits [`XLEN-1`:`first_extended_bit`] of the return value
should get the value of bit (`first_extended bit - 1`).
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `XReg`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `XReg`
! value
! 1
h! `XReg`
! first_extended_bit
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]first_extended_bit == MXLEN) {
  return value;
} else {
  Bits<1> sign = value[first_extended_bit - 1];
  for pass:[(]U32 i = MXLEN - 1; i >= first_extended_bit; i--) {
    value[i] = sign;
  }
  return value;
}
----

[#udb-function-softfloat_addMagsF32]
=== softfloat_addMagsF32
Returns sum of the magnitudes of 2 floating point numbers
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `U32`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `U32`
! a
! 1
h! `U32`
! b
! 2
h! `RoundingMode`
! mode
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-is_sp_signaling_nan?[is_sp_signaling_nan?]pass:[(]a) || xref:#udb-function-is_sp_signaling_nan?[is_sp_signaling_nan?]pass:[(]b)) {
  xref:#udb-function-set_fp_flag[set_fp_flag]pass:[(]FpFlag::NV);
}
Bits<16> expA = xref:#udb-function-expF32UI[expF32UI]pass:[(]a);
Bits<32> sigA = xref:#udb-function-fracF32UI[fracF32UI]pass:[(]a);
Bits<16> expB = xref:#udb-function-expF32UI[expF32UI]pass:[(]b);
Bits<32> sigB = xref:#udb-function-fracF32UI[fracF32UI]pass:[(]b);
U32 sigZ;
U32 z;
Bits<1> signZ;
Bits<8> expZ;
Bits<16> expDiff = expA - expB;
if pass:[(]expDiff == 16'd0) {
  if pass:[(]expA == 8'd0) {
    z = a pass:[+] b;
    return z;
  }
  if pass:[(]expA == 8'hFF) {
    if pass:[(](sigA != 8'd0) || (sigB != 8'd0)) {
      return SP_CANONICAL_NAN;
    }
    return a;
  }
  signZ = xref:#udb-function-signF32UI[signF32UI]pass:[(]a);
  expZ = expA;
  sigZ = 32'h01000000 pass:[+] sigA pass:[+] sigB;
  if pass:[(]((sigZ & 0x1) == 0) && (expZ < 8'hFE)) {
    sigZ = sigZ >> 1;
    return (32'h0 pass:[+] (signZ << 31) pass:[+] (expZ << 23) pass:[+] sigZ);
  }
  sigZ = sigZ << 6;
} else {
  signZ = xref:#udb-function-signF32UI[signF32UI]pass:[(]a);
  sigA = sigA << 6;
  sigB = sigB << 6;
  if pass:[(]$signed+++(+++expDiff) < 0s) {
    if pass:[(]expB == 8'hFF) {
      if pass:[(]sigB != 0) {
        return SP_CANONICAL_NAN;
      }
      return xref:#udb-function-packToF32UI[packToF32UI]pass:[(]signZ, 8'hFF, 23'h0);
    }
    expZ = expB;
    sigA = (expA == 0) ? 2 * sigA : (sigA pass:[+] 0x20000000);
    sigA = xref:#udb-function-softfloat_shiftRightJam32[softfloat_shiftRightJam32]pass:[(]sigA, -expDiff);
  } else {
    if pass:[(]expA == 8'hFF) {
      if pass:[(]sigA != 0) {
        return SP_CANONICAL_NAN;
      }
      return a;
    }
    expZ = expA;
    sigB = (expB == 0) ? 2 * sigB : (sigB pass:[+] 0x20000000);
    sigB = xref:#udb-function-softfloat_shiftRightJam32[softfloat_shiftRightJam32]pass:[(]sigB, expDiff);
  }
  sigZ = 0x20000000 pass:[+] sigA pass:[+] sigB;
  if pass:[(]sigZ < 0x40000000) {
    expZ = expZ - 1;
    sigZ = sigZ << 1;
  }
}
return xref:#udb-function-softfloat_roundPackToF32[softfloat_roundPackToF32]pass:[(]signZ, expZ, sigZ, mode);
----

[#udb-function-softfloat_approxRecip32_1]
=== softfloat_approxRecip32_1
Returns floor(0x7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFF / a), matching the platform-specific
Berkeley SoftFloat macro on 64-bit hosts. Computed via binary long division.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `U32`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<32>`
! a
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<64> a64 = {32'h0, a};
Bits<64> a_top = a64 << 31;
Bits<64> rem = 64'h7FFFFFFFFFFFFFFF;
Bits<32> q = 0;
for pass:[(]U32 i = 0; i < 32; i++) {
  Bits<64> d = a_top >> i;
  if pass:[(]rem >= d) {
    q = q | (32'h1 << (31 - i));
    rem = rem - d;
  }
}
return q;
----

[#udb-function-softfloat_approxRecipSqrt32_1]
=== softfloat_approxRecipSqrt32_1
Returns a 32-bit approximation of 1/sqrt(a).
Translated from Berkeley Softfloat softfloat_approxRecipSqrt32_1.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `U32`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<1>`
! oddExpA
! 1
h! `Bits<32>`
! a
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<8> index = ((a >> 27) & 0xE) pass:[+] oddExpA;
Bits<16> eps = a >> 12;
Bits<16> r0 = softfloat_approxRecipSqrt_1k0s[index] - ((softfloat_approxRecipSqrt_1k1s[index] pass:[`]* eps) >> 20);
Bits<32> eSqrR0 = r0 pass:[`]* r0;
if pass:[(]oddExpA == 0) {
  eSqrR0 = eSqrR0 << 1;
}
Bits<32> sigma0 = ~((eSqrR0 pass:[`]* a) >> 23);
Bits<32> r = (r0 pass:[`]<< 16) pass:[+] ((r0 pass:[`]* sigma0) >> 25);
Bits<32> sqrSigma0 = (sigma0 pass:[`]* sigma0) >> 32;
Bits<32> adj = ((r >> 1) pass:[+] (r >> 3) - (r0 pass:[`]<< 14));
r = r pass:[+] ((adj pass:[`]* sqrSigma0) >> 48);
if pass:[(](r & 32'h80000000) == 0) {
  r = 32'h80000000;
}
return r;
----

[#udb-function-softfloat_normSubnormalF32Sig]
=== softfloat_normSubnormalF32Sig
Normalize a subnormal F32 significand.
Returns (exp, sig32) where exp is the adjusted exponent (two's complement
in Bits<16>) and sig32 is the normalized significand with implicit leading 1
at bit 23. The exponent is widened to Bits<16> (rather than the natural
Bits<8>) so that callers can add two adjusted exponents together (e.g.
expA + expB - bias) without overflow.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `ExpAndSig`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<32>`
! sig
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<16> shiftDist = xref:#udb-function-count_leading_zeros[count_leading_zeros]pass:[(]32, sig) - 8;
ExpAndSig ret;
ret.exp = 1 - shiftDist;
ret.sig = sig << shiftDist;
return ret;
----

[#udb-function-softfloat_roundPackToF32]
=== softfloat_roundPackToF32
Round FP value according to +mdode+ and then pack it in IEEE format.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `Bits<32>`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<1>`
! sign
! 1
h! `Bits<16>`
! exp
! 2
h! `Bits<32>`
! sig
! 3
h! `RoundingMode`
! mode
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Bits<8> roundIncrement = 0x40;
if pass:[(](mode != RoundingMode::RNE) && (mode != RoundingMode::RMM)) {
  roundIncrement = (mode == ((sign != 0) ? RoundingMode::RDN : RoundingMode::RUP)) ? 0x7F : 0;
}
Bits<8> roundBits = sig & 0x7f;
if pass:[(]0xFD <= exp) {
  if pass:[(]$signed+++(+++exp) < 's0) {
    Boolean isTiny = ($signed+++(+++exp) < -8's1) || (sig pass:[+] roundIncrement < 0x80000000);
    sig = xref:#udb-function-softfloat_shiftRightJam32[softfloat_shiftRightJam32]pass:[(]sig, -exp);
    exp = 0;
    roundBits = sig & 0x7F;
    if pass:[(]isTiny && (roundBits != 0)) {
      xref:#udb-function-set_fp_flag[set_fp_flag]pass:[(]FpFlag::UF);
    }
  } else if pass:[(]'shFD < $signed+++(+++exp) || (0x80000000 <= sig pass:[+] roundIncrement)) {
    xref:#udb-function-set_fp_flag[set_fp_flag]pass:[(]FpFlag::OF);
    xref:#udb-function-set_fp_flag[set_fp_flag]pass:[(]FpFlag::NX);

sig = (sig pass:[+] roundIncrement) >> 7;
if pass:[(]roundBits != 0) {

  sig = sig & ~32'b1;

[#udb-function-softfloat_roundPackToF32_no_flag]
=== softfloat_roundPackToF32_no_flag
Round FP value according to +mdode+ and then pack it in IEEE format.
No flags to be set
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `Bits<32>`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]

! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<1>`
! sign
! 1
h! `Bits<16>`
! exp
! 2
h! `Bits<32>`
! sig
! 3
h! `RoundingMode`
! mode
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]

Bits<8> roundIncrement = 0x40;
if pass:[(](mode != RoundingMode::RNE) && (mode != RoundingMode::RMM)) {
  roundIncrement = (mode == ((sign != 0) ? RoundingMode::RDN : RoundingMode::RUP)) ? 0x7F : 0;
}
Bits<8> roundBits = sig & 0x7f;
if pass:[(]0xFD <= exp) {
  if pass:[(]$signed+++(+++exp) < 's0) {
    Boolean isTiny = ($signed+++(+++exp) < -8's1) || (sig pass:[+] roundIncrement < 0x80000000);
    sig = xref:#udb-function-softfloat_shiftRightJam32[softfloat_shiftRightJam32]pass:[(]sig, -exp);
    exp = 0;
    roundBits = sig & 0x7F;
  } else if pass:[(]'shFD < $signed+++(+++exp) || (0x80000000 <= sig pass:[+] roundIncrement)) {
    return xref:#udb-function-packToF32UI[packToF32UI]pass:[(]sign, 0xFF, 0) - ((roundIncrement == 0) ? 1 : 0);
  }
}
sig = (sig pass:[+] roundIncrement) >> 7;
sig = sig & ~((roundBits ^ 0x40) & ((mode == RoundingMode::RNE) ? 1 : 0));
if pass:[(]sig == 0) {
  exp = 0;
}
return xref:#udb-function-packToF32UI[packToF32UI]pass:[(]sign, exp, sig);

  return sign == 1 ? WORD_NEG_OVERFLOW : WORD_POS_OVERFLOW;
}
if pass:[(]roundBits != 0) {

}
return z;

[#udb-function-softfloat_shiftRightJam32]
=== softfloat_shiftRightJam32
Shifts +a+ right by the number of bits given in +dist+, which must not
be zero.  If any nonzero bits are shifted off, they are "jammed" into the
least-significant bit of the shifted value by setting the least-significant
bit to 1.  This shifted-and-jammed value is returned.
The value of +dist+ can be arbitrarily large.  In particular, if +dist+ is
greater than 32, the result will be either 0 or 1, depending on whether +a+
is zero or nonzero.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `Bits<32>`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<32>`
! a
! 1
h! `Bits<32>`
! dist
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
return (dist < 31) ? a >> dist | ((a[dist - 1:0] != 0) ? 1 : 0) : ((a != 0) ? 1 : 0);
----

[#udb-function-softfloat_shiftRightJam64]
=== softfloat_shiftRightJam64
Shifts +a+ right by the number of bits given in +dist+, which must not
be zero.  If any nonzero bits are shifted off, they are "jammed" into the
least-significant bit of the shifted value by setting the least-significant
bit to 1.  This shifted-and-jammed value is returned.

The value of 'dist' can be arbitrarily large.  In particular, if +dist+ is
greater than 64, the result will be either 0 or 1, depending on whether +a+
is zero or nonzero.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `Bits<64>`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `Bits<64>`
! a
! 1
h! `Bits<32>`
! dist
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
return (dist < 63) ? a >> dist | ((a[dist - 1:0] != 0) ? 1 : 0) : ((a != 0) ? 1 : 0);
----

}
Bits<16> expA = xref:#udb-function-expF32UI[expF32UI]pass:[(]a);
Bits<32> sigA = xref:#udb-function-fracF32UI[fracF32UI]pass:[(]a);
Bits<16> expB = xref:#udb-function-expF32UI[expF32UI]pass:[(]b);
Bits<32> sigB = xref:#udb-function-fracF32UI[fracF32UI]pass:[(]b);
U32 sigZ;
U32 z;
Bits<1> signZ;
Bits<8> expZ;
U32 sigDiff;
U32 sigX;
U32 sigY;
Bits<8> shiftDist;
Bits<16> expDiff = expA - expB;
if pass:[(]expDiff == 16'd0) {
  if pass:[(]expA == 8'hFF) {
    xref:#udb-function-set_fp_flag[set_fp_flag]pass:[(]FpFlag::NV);
    return SP_CANONICAL_NAN;
  }
  sigDiff = sigA - sigB;
  if pass:[(]sigDiff == 0) {
    return xref:#udb-function-packToF32UI[packToF32UI]pass:[(]((mode == RoundingMode::RDN) ? 1 : 0), 0, 0);
  }
  if pass:[(]expA != 0) {
    expA = expA - 1;
  }
  signZ = xref:#udb-function-signF32UI[signF32UI]pass:[(]a);
  if pass:[(]sigDiff < 0) {
    signZ = ~signZ;
    sigDiff = -32'sh1 * sigDiff;
  }
  shiftDist = xref:#udb-function-count_leading_zeros[count_leading_zeros]pass:[(]32, sigDiff) - 8;
  expZ = expA - shiftDist;
  if pass:[(]expZ < 0) {
    shiftDist = expA;
    expZ = 0;
  }
  return xref:#udb-function-packToF32UI[packToF32UI]pass:[(]signZ, expZ, sigDiff << shiftDist);
} else {
  signZ = xref:#udb-function-signF32UI[signF32UI]pass:[(]a);
  sigA = sigA << 7;
  sigB = sigB << 7;
  if pass:[(]$signed+++(+++expDiff) < 0s) {
    signZ = ~signZ;
    if pass:[(]expB == 0xFF) {
      if pass:[(]sigB != 0) {
        return SP_CANONICAL_NAN;
      }
      return xref:#udb-function-packToF32UI[packToF32UI]pass:[(]signZ, expB, 0);
    }
    expZ = expB - 1;
    sigX = sigB | 0x40000000;
    sigY = sigA pass:[+] ((expA != 0) ? 0x40000000 : sigA);
    expDiff = -expDiff;
  } else {
    if pass:[(]expA == 0xFF) {
      if pass:[(]sigA != 0) {
        return SP_CANONICAL_NAN;
      }
      return a;
    }
    expZ = expA - 1;
    sigX = sigA | 0x40000000;
    sigY = sigB pass:[+] ((expB != 0) ? 0x40000000 : sigB);
  }
  return xref:#udb-function-softfloat_normRoundPackToF32[softfloat_normRoundPackToF32]pass:[(]signZ, expZ, sigX - xref:#udb-function-softfloat_shiftRightJam32[softfloat_shiftRightJam32]pass:[(]sigY, expDiff), mode);

[#udb-function-translate]
=== translate
Translate a virtual address for operation type +op+ that appears to execute at
+effective_mode+.

The translation will depend on the effective privilege mode.

May raise a Page Fault or Access Fault.

The final physical address is *not* access checked (for PMP, PMA, etc., violations).
(though intermediate page table reads will be)
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `TranslationResult`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `XReg`
! vaddr
! 1
h! `MemoryOperation`
! op
! 2
h! `PrivilegeMode`
! effective_mode
! 3
h! `Bits<INSTR_ENC_SIZE>`
! encoding
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
Boolean cached_translation_valid;
CachedTranslationResult cached_translation_result;
cached_translation_result = xref:#udb-function-cached_translation[cached_translation]pass:[(]vaddr, op);
if pass:[(]cached_translation_result.valid) {
  return cached_translation_result.result;
}
TranslationResult result;
if pass:[(]effective_mode == PrivilegeMode::M) {
  result.paddr = vaddr;
  return result;
}
SatpMode translation_mode = xref:#udb-function-current_translation_mode[current_translation_mode]pass:[(]effective_mode);
if pass:[(]translation_mode == SatpMode::Reserved) {
  if pass:[(]op == MemoryOperation::Read) {
    xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::LoadPageFault, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), vaddr);
  } else if pass:[(]op == MemoryOperation::Write || op == MemoryOperation::ReadModifyWrite) {
    xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::StoreAmoPageFault, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), vaddr);
  } else {
    xref:#udb-function-assert[assert]pass:[(]op == MemoryOperation::Fetch, "Unexpected memory operation");
    xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::InstructionPageFault, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), vaddr);
  }
}
if pass:[(]translation_mode == SatpMode::Bare) {
  result.paddr = vaddr;
} else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-xlen[xlen]pass:[(]) == 32 && translation_mode == SatpMode::Sv32) {
  result = xref:#udb-function-stage1_page_walk[stage1_page_walk]pass:[(]32, 34, 32, 2, vaddr, op, effective_mode, encoding);
} else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-xlen[xlen]pass:[(]) == 64 && translation_mode == SatpMode::Sv39) {
  result = xref:#udb-function-stage1_page_walk[stage1_page_walk]pass:[(]39, 56, 64, 3, vaddr, op, effective_mode, encoding);
} else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-xlen[xlen]pass:[(]) == 64 && translation_mode == SatpMode::Sv48) {
  result = xref:#udb-function-stage1_page_walk[stage1_page_walk]pass:[(]48, 56, 64, 4, vaddr, op, effective_mode, encoding);
} else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-xlen[xlen]pass:[(]) == 64 && translation_mode == SatpMode::Sv57) {
  result = xref:#udb-function-stage1_page_walk[stage1_page_walk]pass:[(]57, 56, 64, 5, vaddr, op, effective_mode, encoding);
} else {
  xref:#udb-function-assert[assert]pass:[(]false, "Unexpected SatpMode");
  xref:#udb-function-unreachable[unreachable]pass:[(]);
}
xref:#udb-function-maybe_cache_translation[maybe_cache_translation]pass:[(]vaddr, op, result);
return result;
----

[#udb-function-unpredictable]
=== unpredictable (builtin)
Indicate that the hart has reached a state that is unpredictable because the
RISC-V spec allows multiple behaviors. Generally, this will be a fatal condition
to any emulation, since it is unclear what to do next.

The single argument _why_ is a string describing why the hart entered an
unpredictable state.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `void`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `String`
! why
!===

|===


[#udb-function-vector_state]
=== vector_state
Get the current vector state from CSRs
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `VectorState`
h| Arguments
a|

None

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
VectorState state;
state.log2_sew = 3 pass:[+] CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vtype[vtype]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vtype-VSEW[VSEW];
state.sew = 7'b1 << state.log2_sew;
Bits<3> vlmul = CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vtype[vtype]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vtype-VLMUL[VLMUL];
state.lmul_type = CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vtype[vtype]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vtype-VLMUL[VLMUL][2] == 1'b1 ? VectorLmulType::Divide : VectorLmulType::Multiply;
state.log2_lmul = CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vtype[vtype]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vtype-VLMUL[VLMUL][1:0];
if pass:[(]vlmul == 3'b101) {
  state.log2_lmul = 3;
} else if pass:[(]vlmul == 3'b110) {
  state.log2_lmul = 2;
} else if pass:[(]vlmul == 3'b111) {
  state.log2_lmul = 1;
} else if pass:[(]vlmul == 3'b100) {
  xref:#udb-function-unpredictable[unpredictable]pass:[(]"VLMUL value 0b100 is reserved");
}
return state;
----

  xref:#udb-function-raise[raise]pass:[(]ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAddressMisaligned, xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]), virtual_address);
} else {
  if pass:[(]MISALIGNED_SPLIT_STRATEGY == "sequential_bytes") {
    for pass:[(]U32 I = 0; I < (LEN / 8); I++) {
      xref:#udb-function-write_memory_aligned[write_memory_aligned]pass:[(]8, virtual_address pass:[+] I, (value >> (8 * I))[7:0], encoding, 1'b0, 1'b0);
    }
  } else if pass:[(]MISALIGNED_SPLIT_STRATEGY == "custom") {
    xref:#udb-function-unpredictable[unpredictable]pass:[(]"An implementation is free to break a misaligned access any way, leading to unpredictable behavior when any part of the misaligned access causes an exception");
  }
}
----

[#udb-function-write_memory_aligned]
=== write_memory_aligned
Write to virtual memory using a known aligned address.

If +rl+ is 1, then the store also acts as a memory model release (applied after all
checks pass but before the physical write).
If +aq+ is 1, then the store also acts as a memory model acquire.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `void`
h| Arguments
a|

[cols="1,4,4"]
!===
! Idx ! Type ! Name

! 0
h! `U32`
! LEN
! 1
h! `XReg`
! virtual_address
! 2
h! `XReg`
! value
! 3
h! `Bits<INSTR_ENC_SIZE>`
! encoding
! 4
h! `Bits<1>`
! aq
! 5
h! `Bits<1>`
! rl
!===

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
XReg physical_address;
physical_address = (CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-S[S] == 1) ? xref:#udb-function-translate[translate]pass:[(]virtual_address, MemoryOperation::Write, xref:#udb-function-effective_ldst_mode[effective_ldst_mode]pass:[(]), encoding).paddr : virtual_address;
xref:#udb-function-access_check[access_check]pass:[(]physical_address, LEN, virtual_address, MemoryOperation::Write, ExceptionCode::StoreAmoAccessFault, xref:#udb-function-effective_ldst_mode[effective_ldst_mode]pass:[(]));
if pass:[(]rl == 1'b1) {
  xref:#udb-function-memory_model_release[memory_model_release]pass:[(]);
}
xref:#udb-function-write_physical_memory[write_physical_memory]pass:[(]LEN, physical_address, value);
if pass:[(]aq == 1'b1) {
  xref:#udb-function-memory_model_acquire[memory_model_acquire]pass:[(]);
}
----

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]len == 8) {
  xref:#udb-function-write_physical_memory_8[write_physical_memory_8]pass:[(]paddr, value);
} else if pass:[(]len == 16) {
  xref:#udb-function-write_physical_memory_16[write_physical_memory_16]pass:[(]paddr, value);
} else if pass:[(]len == 32) {
  xref:#udb-function-write_physical_memory_32[write_physical_memory_32]pass:[(]paddr, value);
} else if pass:[(]len == 64) {
  xref:#udb-function-write_physical_memory_64[write_physical_memory_64]pass:[(]paddr, value);
} else {
  xref:#udb-function-assert[assert]pass:[(]false, "Invalid len");
}

[#udb-function-xlen]
=== xlen
Returns the effective XLEN for the current privilege mode.
  

[cols="1,4"]
|===
h| Return Type h| `Bits<8>`
h| Arguments
a|

None

|===

[source,idl,subs="specialchars,macros"]
----
if pass:[(]MXLEN == 32) {
  return 32;
} else {
  if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::M) {
    if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-MXL[MXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN32)) {
      return 32;
    } else if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-misa[misa]].xref:#udb-csrfield-misa-MXL[MXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
      return 64;
    } else {
      xref:#udb-function-unreachable[unreachable]pass:[(]);
    }
  } else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::S) && xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::S) {
    if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-SXL[SXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN32)) {
      return 32;
    } else if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-SXL[SXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
      return 64;
    } else {
      xref:#udb-function-unreachable[unreachable]pass:[(]);
    }
  } else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::U) && xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::U) {
    if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-UXL[UXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN32)) {
      return 32;
    } else if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-mstatus[mstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-mstatus-UXL[UXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
      return 64;
    } else {
      xref:#udb-function-unreachable[unreachable]pass:[(]);
    }
  } else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::H) && xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::VS) {
    if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-VSXL[VSXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN32)) {
      return 32;
    } else if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-hstatus[hstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-hstatus-VSXL[VSXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
      return 64;
    } else {
      xref:#udb-function-unreachable[unreachable]pass:[(]);
    }
  } else if pass:[(]xref:#udb-function-implemented?[implemented?]pass:[(]ExtensionName::H) && xref:#udb-function-mode[mode]pass:[(]) == PrivilegeMode::VU) {
    if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vsstatus[vsstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vsstatus-UXL[UXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN32)) {
      return 32;
    } else if pass:[(]CSR[xref:#udb-csr-vsstatus[vsstatus]].xref:#udb-csrfield-vsstatus-UXL[UXL] == $bits(XRegWidth::XLEN64)) {
      return 64;
    } else {
      xref:#udb-function-unreachable[unreachable]pass:[(]);
    }
  } else {
    xref:#udb-function-unreachable[unreachable]pass:[(]);
  }
}
